# MAT3 (GameCube) -> N64 data-symbol map (lane w36y, wave 36)

Generated by `cloud/work/frontier/w36y/tools/output.py`; machine-readable rows in `map_data.json`. Not applied anywhere. Overlay addresses (0x8038A400..) are per image (population ovl_a / ovl_b).

Placement channels: **code** (aligned global references of w36n proven/strong function pairs) and **content** (the GC object's initial bytes found uniquely in the N64 image). Precision against the identities other lanes stated (tools/evaluate.py): see RESULTS.md. Use proven rows as identities, strong rows as very likely, lead rows as hints. ALWAYS take field offsets from the N64 column / types.md, not from GC. A third channel adds identities other lanes stated (column `lane` in the JSON). `vol` is a heuristic (confirmed for gSeconds, gFrameSeconds, gNumCars, gNumModels). Struct layouts: types.md.

| population | proven | strong | lead |
|---|---|---|---|
| static-data | 10 | 3 | 7 |
| game | 359 | 179 | 196 |
| ovl_a | 128 | 86 | 43 |
| ovl_b | 30 | 14 | 6 |

Columns: `fn` = distinct N64 functions whose aligned references vote this base (GC functions in parentheses), `unm` = unmatched N64 functions that reference the object, `vol` = IDO volatile pattern (per-use formed address + 0(reg) access), `layout` = GC field offset -> N64 offset where they differ (from aligned accesses).
